Why the outcome differs

  • judge was cheaper by $0.4398 (baseline $1.3014 vs judge $0.8616).
  • The judge flagged 5 diff(s) as advisory without blocking them — every one was allowed through, so it added review overhead, not a hard stop.
  • Both sides opened a PR; compare the diffs directly to judge whether the extra judge/reviewer cost bought a better patch.

PR review

Both sides shipped the same nominal feature (settings modal: theme switcher + one toggle). Scored by actually building/vet-ing/testing each PR's branch and reading the diffs — not the agents' own self-reports, which turned out to matter (see below).

PR #4 — baseline 7.5/10

what's good
  • Verified independently: go build ./... and go vet ./... both pass clean on the actual PR branch.
  • internal/tui/keys.go: the global switch (which includes the new , Settings binding) sits after the if m.focus == focusComposer block, which returns early (handled=false) for anything it doesn't explicitly claim — so typing a literal comma while composing a message is correctly not intercepted. This is the exact bug class earlier runs shipped; this one avoids it.
  • Cursor wraparound uses distinct cursor-- / cursor++ expressions (not the same expression for both directions, another bug seen in earlier runs).
  • Updated plan.md's keybinding cheatsheet with the new shortcut.
  • The coder's own self-reported verdict ("go build, go vet, go test ./internal/tui/... all pass") matches what independent verification found — its self-check was trustworthy this time.
issues
  • Repo pollution: the PR includes .engine/memory.json as a new file — an internal engine bookkeeping artifact that has no business in the target repository's git history. .engine/ isn't in this repo's .gitignore either, so nothing caught it.
  • No new test covers the settings modal's own logic (composer-guard, wraparound, persistence round-trip) — app_test.go was only touched to update an unrelated call site's new bool parameter.
  • Minor: saveSettingsNow() is called on every single arrow-key press while cycling themes, not just on close — harmless but more disk I/O than needed.
  • No true "cancel" semantics — esc always commits whatever you last landed on. Simpler and internally consistent (see judge's PR for the alternative failure mode), but means there's no preview-then-discard.

PR #5 — judge 5.0/10

what's good
  • Settings bound to ctrl+s — a control combo, structurally immune to the composer-typing collision class of bug by construction, no focus-guard logic required.
  • Cursor wraparound is correct (asymmetric +1/-1 expressions).
  • Cleaner separation of concerns: persistence logic lives in its own internal/settings package (exported Load/Save/Default) rather than inline in tui.
  • Extra polish baseline lacks: glamourStyleForTheme() switches the markdown renderer's own style between light/dark to match the active theme.
  • No stray files committed — clean diff, nothing outside the feature.
issues
  • Verified build-breaking regression: tui.New()'s signature grew a 4th settings.Settings parameter, but internal/tui/app_test.go was never updated — go vet ./... and go test ./... both fail to even compile, at 7 separate call sites (app_test.go:13,27,47,79,96,118,168). Confirmed by cloning the actual PR branch and running the toolchain directly, not by trusting the coder's report.
  • Broken cancel semantics: the settings modal's own hint text says "esc cancel", and esc does revert the theme (applyTheme(m.settingsOrigTheme)) — but the mouse-support toggle is never reverted. Its live tea.EnableMouseCellMotion/tea.DisableMouse command already fired and stays in effect, and m.settings.MouseEnabled is never reset, so "cancel" only cancels half of what it claims to.
  • The coder's own final report is just a stray error string — its recorded outcome field is literally "error: [Errno 8] nodename nor servname provided, or not known" (the same DNS-resolution failure seen elsewhere in this run's logs), not a real summary. That same string is what ended up as the PR's title and description on GitHub — there is no human-readable account of what this PR does on the PR itself, and no self-verification claim survived to cross-check against.
  • No documentation update (plan.md untouched).
  • No new tests for the settings feature (same gap as baseline).
verdict

Baseline's PR is the one I'd actually merge as-is (after dropping the stray .engine/memory.json file): it builds, vets, and its test suite still compiles, with correct composer-focus and cursor-wraparound handling. Judge's PR has the nicer architecture and small UX polish, but ships a genuinely broken test suite (go vet/go test fail to compile) and a half-implemented cancel path — and the fact that its own agent's final report was silently replaced by an unrelated network error means nothing in the PR's own trail can be trusted to have caught either problem. The cost/speed win judge showed in the metrics above ($0.86 vs $1.30) does not reflect a better patch here — it reflects less verification work actually happening, not more efficient verification.

Baseline — judge off

I've kicked off an investigation into the codebase to understand the TUI framework, existing theming, modal patterns, keybindings, and settings persistence. I'll follow up with an implementation plan once that comes back.

ok ask 08553d06 $0.215 · 215,471 tok

What: reconnaissance for adding a settings modal (theme switcher + options) to the codeloom-tui Go/Bubble Tea app.

  1. Framework/language: Go (module github.com/iresharma/codeloom.TUI, go.mod at repo root). Charm stack: github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea v1.3.10 (Elm-architecture TUI framework), bubbles v1.0.0 (widgets: textinput, textarea, viewport, help, spinner, progress), lipgloss v1.1.0 (styling), glamour (markdown render, used in fileview/chat), lrstanley/bubblezone (mouse hit-zones). All TUI code lives in internal/tui/.

2. Entry points:
- cmd/codeloom-tui/main.go: process entry. Builds tui.New(workspace, eng, conn) then tea.NewProgram(model, tea.WithAltScreen(), tea.WithMouseCellMotion()), runs p.Run(), calls final.(tui.Model).Close().
- internal/tui/app.go: type Model struct {...} is the bubbletea root model (Model.Init, Model.Update, and Model.View — View is in layout.go). func New(workspace string, eng *host.Engine, conn *client.Conn) Model (app.go:125) constructs the model incl. all bubbles widgets and default field values — this is where you'd add settings-model init/defaults.
- Model.Update (app.go:256) is the tea.Msg dispatch switch (WindowSizeMsg, tickMsg, spinner.TickMsg, errMsg, eventMsg, tea.MouseMsg, tea.KeyMsg). Key handling for tea.KeyMsg delegates to m.handleKey(msg) in keys.go.

3. Theming: There is NO theme system, no theme list, no config file today — it's a single hardcoded palette.
- internal/tui/styles.go (77 lines) declares package-level lipgloss.Color vars: bg, bgAlt, fg, dim, accent, green, red, yellow, magenta, border, focusC (Tokyo-Night-like hex values, e.g. accent = "#7aa2f7"), plus a shared zones = zone.New() bubblezone registry, and style helper funcs: paneStyle(focused bool, w, h int), titleStyle(), dimStyle(), gitMod/gitAdd/gitUntracked(), headerBar(text, w), fit(s, w, h), sectionBorder().
- Every other file (chat.go, tabs.go, graph.go, layout.go, context.go, fileview.go, stats.go, app.go) references these same package vars directly (lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(accent) etc.) rather than through an indirection layer/struct. To add theme switching you'll need to turn these vars into either (a) fields on a theme/palette struct held on Model and threaded into style helpers, or (b) mutable package vars swapped on selection — but since style-helper funcs (dimStyle(), paneStyle(), titleStyle(), etc.) already centralize a lot of usage, the cleanest low-risk approach is to keep styles.go's helper functions but back them with a mutable current-theme struct instead of consts, and expose a small themes.go with named palettes (e.g. "Tokyo Night" = current values, plus a couple more) and a setTheme(name) that reassigns the package vars/struct fields. No color usage is scattered as literal hexes outside styles.go — confirmed via grep across the tui package.
- No persisted config file exists anywhere in the repo (grepped for config|Config|persist|json.Marshal|os.UserConfigDir|yaml — only hit is protocol.go:78 marshalling wire messages, unrelated). Settings today are all in-memory-only Model fields (no ~/.config/codeloom or similar).

4. Existing modal/popup patterns to copy — there are actually three distinct "overlay" styles already in the codebase, all rendered via the same overlay() helper:
- internal/tui/layout.go:219 func overlay(w, h int, _, box string) string — centers box over the full screen using lipgloss.Place(..., lipgloss.Center, lipgloss.Center, box, WithWhitespaceForeground(dim)). Called from View() (layout.go:86-105): if m.help.ShowAll { ui = overlay(...) } and if m.modal != modalNone { ui = overlay(...) }. This is the mechanism to reuse for a settings modal: add if m.settingsOpen { ui = overlay(m.width, m.height, ui, m.viewSettings()) } near the other two, ideally after them so settings renders on top (or decide ordering/exclusivity).
- Simple text-input modal (create/rename/delete file), the most directly reusable pattern for a list/selection dialog:
- Enum modalKind + consts in app.go:54-62 (modalNone, modalCreateFile, modalCreateDir, modalRename, modalDelete) — add a new modalSettings (or a wholly separate bool field, see below) kind here if you want to fold settings into this same enum, though settings needs richer state (selected theme index etc.) than the single modalInput textinput.Model this system carries.
- State on Model (app.go:113-118): modal modalKind, modalPath string, plus reuse of modalInput textinput.Model (app.go:89).
- Open: func (m *Model) openModal(kind modalKind, initial, path string) (app.go:406) sets kind/path, seeds/focuses modalInput.
- Key routing: handleKey (keys.go:152) short-circuits to m.handleModalKey(msg) whenever m.modal != modalNone (checked first, before Quit/Help/anything else) — a settings modal should follow the same "intercept all keys while open" pattern.
- func (m *Model) handleModalKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) tea.Cmd (app.go:414): esc closes, enter calls commitModal(), else forwards keystrokes into m.modalInput.Update(msg).
- func (m *Model) commitModal() tea.Cmd (app.go:428): switches on kind, resets modal state, sends a protocol.Command to the engine (not needed for settings, which is local-only).
- Rendering: func (m Model) viewModal() string (layout.go:226-246): switch on m.modal builds a title/hint string, then lipgloss.NewStyle().Border(RoundedBorder()).BorderForeground(accent).Padding(1,2).Width(min(60,max(24,m.width-4))).Render(body). Good template for a bordered card; for settings you'd render a list of options with a cursor instead of a single textinput.
- Where opened from: tree key handler handleTreeKey (app.go:473-519), e.g. case "a": m.openModal(modalCreateFile, "", "").
- Selection-list-with-cursor pattern (closer to what a theme picker needs; no textinput, arrow-key cursor over a slice of choices), found in the "prompt card" (agent asking user to choose) — internal/tui/chat.go:
- State: promptChoices []string, promptCursor int (app.go:115-118).
- Render: func (m Model) viewPromptCard(w int) string (chat.go:111-144) — loops for i, c := range m.promptChoices, renders ❯ marker + accent-bold style for the selected row vs dimStyle() for others, and wraps each row in zones.Mark("prompt-"+fmt.Sprint(i), ...) for mouse click support.
- Move cursor: func (m *Model) movePromptChoice(delta int) (chat.go:146) — wraps around with modulo-like clamp logic.
- Key handling: func (m *Model) handlePromptKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) (tea.Cmd, bool) (keys.go:247-288) — up/down or j/k (when input empty) move cursor, enter selects, esc declines, digit keys 1-9 jump-select.
- Mouse handling for the same widget: app.go:351-359 in handleMouse, using zones.Get("prompt-"+strconv.Itoa(i)).InBounds(msg).
- This list+cursor+zones pattern (not the textinput one) is the best structural match for a theme-select list inside a settings modal.
- Full-help overlay (func (m Model) viewFullHelp() string, layout.go:208-217) is a third, simplest example: a bordered box wrapping m.help.View(m), toggled by ? key (m.help.ShowAll), closed by ? or esc. Good minimal template if settings is just a static info/help-like panel rather than interactive controls.

5. Keybindings — centralized in internal/tui/keys.go:
- type keySet struct { ... } (keys.go:27-40) holds all key.Binding fields; func newKeySet() keySet (keys.go:42-76) constructs them with key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(...), key.WithHelp(...)). Add a new field e.g. Settings key.Binding here, bound to e.g. "," or "ctrl+," or "s" (currently unused top-level letters: check collisions — s isn't bound globally, but is worth double-checking against pane-specific handlers like tree's r/d/a/A). Model.keys keySet is stored on the model (app.go:77) and initialized in New() via keys: newKeySet() (app.go:189).
- Global dispatch order in func (m *Model) handleKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) (cmd tea.Cmd, handled bool) (keys.go:152-245):
1. If m.modal != modalNone → all keys go to handleModalKey (settings-as-modal should be added as an early check here too, e.g. if m.settingsOpen { return m.handleSettingsKey(msg), true }, placed before or alongside the existing modal check depending on desired exclusivity with other modals).
2. Global keys: Quit (ctrl+c), Help (?), Abort (ctrl+x) — checked unconditionally next.
3. If help is showing (m.help.ShowAll), only esc closes it, everything else swallowed.
4. handlePromptKey intercepts if an engine prompt is active.
5. Composer-focused keys.
6. Global pane-switch keys (Tree/Code/Chat/GraphPane/Inspect/NextPane/PrevPane/Insert/Undo) — this is the natural place to add case key.Matches(msg, k.Settings): m.settingsOpen = true; return nil, true (a new binding here, analogous to how Inspect/Zoom are handled, keys.go:224-229).
7. Focus-specific handlers (handleTreeKey, handleCodeKey, handleGraphKey, handleInspectKey).
- Help screen: Model implements help.KeyMap via ShortHelp() []key.Binding (keys.go:78-105) and FullHelp() [][]key.Binding (keys.go:107-116), consumed by the bubbles help.Model (m.help, initialized in New() app.go:175-181, rendered in viewFooter()/viewFullHelp() layout.go:174-217). To surface the new settings key in ? help, add k.Settings into the appropriate group in FullHelp() (and optionally ShortHelp()'s default base slice, keys.go:86).
- plan.md (repo root) has a human-readable "Keys" section (lines 22-29) documenting the current bindings/mouse zones — update it too for consistency, though it's just docs, not code.

  1. Persistence: none. No settings/config file exists (verified: no os.UserConfigDir, no .codeloom rc/json/yaml/toml anywhere in the repo besides the unrelated wire-protocol json.Marshal in internal/protocol/protocol.go:78). Everything settings-like today (theme colors, focus state, chatFollow, etc.) lives purely as in-memory Model fields set at New() and mutated at runtime; nothing is written to disk on quit (Model.Close() at app.go:825 only closes the client conn / kills owned engine process, no config flush). Implementing persistence will require writing new code from scratch, e.g. a internal/tui/settings.go (or internal/config) that reads/writes a JSON file such as filepath.Join(os.UserConfigDir(), "codeloom-tui", "settings.json") (or workspace-local .codeloom/settings.json, mirroring how host.AttachOrSpawn uses {workspace}/.engine/) and is loaded in main.go before tui.New(...) / saved from Model.Close() or immediately on change.

Verdict: Straightforward to add. Concrete plan for a coder:
- Add type settingsField/settingsOpen bool + selection state to Model (app.go struct, alongside modal/promptChoices fields) — or add modalSettings to the existing modalKind enum in app.go:54-62 if you want it to share the generic modal machinery.
- Add themes.go in internal/tui: define a theme struct mirroring the vars in styles.go, a slice of named presets (keep current Tokyo-Night as default), and change styles.go's package vars into fields of a package-level var current theme (or a field on Model, threaded through render funcs) so switching theme just reassigns current.
- Add k.Settings key.Binding in keys.go's keySet/newKeySet(), wire a case in handleKey's global switch (~keys.go:224) to open it, add early interception (like the m.modal != modalNone check at keys.go:153) so it captures keys while open, and a handleSettingsKey following the up/down/enter/esc pattern of handlePromptKey (keys.go:247) or the modal-list pattern in chat.go's prompt-card.
- Add viewSettings() in layout.go modeled on viewModal()/viewPromptCard(), invoke via overlay() in View() (layout.go:101-103), rendering a bordered lipgloss box listing theme names with a ❯ cursor plus other options (mouse zones optional, mirror zones.Mark("prompt-"+i, ...) if wanted).
- Add persistence: new small config load/save (JSON, os.UserConfigDir()-based or workspace .codeloom/settings.json), load in main.go before constructing tui.New, pass initial theme/options into New(...) (extend its signature or add a SetOptions call after), save on selection change or in Model.Close().

Leftover: No existing precedent for config file naming/location in this repo — coder must choose a convention (suggest ~/.config/codeloom-tui/settings.json via os.UserConfigDir(), or workspace-local to match the engine's .engine/ convention) since none exists to mirror. Nothing else unresolved; all relevant files were read directly.

 16|func (m Model) layout() layout {

 20|	}

 22|		h = 24
 23|	}

213|func (m Model) Init() tea.Cmd {
214|	return tea.Batch(
215|		m.listen(),
216|		m.send(protocol.StartSession(m.workspace, "")),
217|		m.send(protocol.RequestSnapshot(true)),
218|		m.send(protocol.RequestGit()),
219|		m.send(protocol.RequestMemory()),
220|		m.send(protocol.RequestContext("")),
221|		m.spin.Tick,
222|		tick(),
223|	)
224|}
225|
226|func tick() tea.Cmd {
227|	return tea.Tick(4*time.Second, func(t time.Time) tea.Msg { return tickMsg(t) })
228|}
229|
230|func (m Model) listen() tea.Cmd {
231|	if m.events == nil {
232|		return nil
233|	}
234|	ch := m.events
235|	return func() tea.Msg {
236|		ev, ok := <-ch
237|		if !ok {
238|			return errMsg{fmt.Errorf("engine disconnected")}
239|		}
240|		return eventMsg(ev)
241|	}
242|}
243|
244|func (m Model) send(cmd protocol.Command) tea.Cmd {
245|	return func() tea.Msg {
246|		if m.conn == nil {
247|			return errMsg{fmt.Errorf("not connected")}
248|		}
249|		if err := m.conn.Send(cmd); err != nil {
250|			return errMsg{err}
251|		}
252|		return nil
253|	}
254|}
255|
256|func (m Model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
257|	var cmds []tea.Cmd
258|	switch msg := msg.(type) {
259|	case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
260|		m.width, m.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
261|		m.layoutViewports()
262|	case tickMsg:
263|		cmds = append(cmds, tick())
264|	case spinner.TickMsg:

266|		m.spin, cmd = m.spin.Update(msg)

272|		if msg.err != nil {

276|	case eventMsg:

280|		cmds = append(cmds, m.listen())

283|		}
284|	case tea.MouseMsg:
285|		if cmd := m.handleMouse(msg); cmd != nil {
286|			cmds = append(cmds, cmd)
287|		}
288|	case tea.KeyMsg:
289|		cmd, handled := m.handleKey(msg)
290|		if cmd != nil {
291|			cmds = append(cmds, cmd)
292|		}
293|		if handled {

296|		var c tea.Cmd

300|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
301|		case m.focus == focusComposer:
302|			m.input, c = m.input.Update(msg)
303|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
304|			m.layoutViewports()
305|		case m.focus == focusCode:
306|			m.fileVP, c = m.fileVP.Update(msg)
307|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
308|		case m.focus == focusChat:
309|			m.chatVP, c = m.chatVP.Update(msg)
310|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
311|			m.chatFollow = m.chatVP.AtBottom()
312|		case m.focus == focusGraph:
313|			m.graphVP, c = m.graphVP.Update(msg)
314|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
315|		case m.focus == focusInspect:
316|			m.inspectVP, c = m.inspectVP.Update(msg)
317|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
318|		}
319|		return m, tea.Batch(cmds...)
320|	}
321|	return m, tea.Batch(cmds...)

247|func (m *Model) handlePromptKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) (tea.Cmd, bool) {
248|	if m.promptID == "" {
249|		return nil, false
250|	}
251|	if m.focus != focusChat && m.focus != focusComposer {
252|		return nil, false
253|	}
254|	k := m.keys
255|	empty := strings.TrimSpace(m.input.Value()) == ""
256|	switch {
257|	case key.Matches(msg, k.Escape):
258|		if m.focus == focusComposer && !empty {
259|			m.input.Reset()
260|			return nil, true
261|		}
262|		return m.answerPrompt(m.promptDecline()), true
263|	case msg.String() == "enter":
264|		if !empty {
265|			return m.submitComposer(), true
266|		}
267|		if len(m.promptChoices) == 0 {
268|			return nil, true
269|		}
270|		return m.answerPrompt(m.selectedPromptChoice()), true
271|	case msg.String() == "up" || (empty && msg.String() == "k"):
272|		m.movePromptChoice(-1)
273|		return nil, true
274|	case msg.String() == "down" || (empty && msg.String() == "j"):
275|		m.movePromptChoice(1)
276|		return nil, true
277|	}
278|	if empty && len(m.promptChoices) > 0 {
279|		s := msg.String()
280|		if len(s) == 1 && s[0] >= '1' && s[0] <= '9' {
281|			idx := int(s[0] - '1')
282|			if idx < len(m.promptChoices) {
283|				return m.answerPrompt(m.promptChoices[idx]), true
284|			}
285|		}
286|	}
287|	return nil, false
288|}

521|func (m *Model) handleEvent(ev protocol.Event) tea.Cmd {
522|	switch ev.Type {
523|	case "ChatMessageStarted":
524|		id := ev.Str("id")
525|		aid := ev.Str("agent_id")
526|		if aid == "" {
527|			m.chat = append(m.chat, chatLine{id: id, role: ev.Str("role"), agentID: aid})
528|			m.streams[id] = len(m.chat) - 1
529|			m.refreshChat()
530|			break
531|		}
532|		m.appendAgent(aid, ev.Str("role"), "", false)
533|		m.streamAgent[id] = aid
534|	case "ChatMessageDelta":
535|		id := ev.Str("id")
536|		if aid, ok := m.streamAgent[id]; ok {
537|			m.appendAgentDelta(aid, ev.Str("text"))
538|			break
539|		}
540|		if i, ok := m.streams[id]; ok && i < len(m.chat) {
541|			m.chat[i].text += ev.Str("text")
542|			m.refreshChat()
543|		}
544|	case "ChatMessageAdded":
545|		id := ev.Str("id")
546|		aid := ev.Str("agent_id")
547|		if _, ok := m.streamAgent[id]; ok {
548|			delete(m.streamAgent, id)
549|			if aid != "" {
550|				lines := m.agentChat[aid]
551|				if len(lines) > 0 {
552|					lines[len(lines)-1].text = ev.Str("text")
553|					lines[len(lines)-1].role = ev.Str("role")
554|					m.agentChat[aid] = lines
555|					if m.chatFilter == aid {
556|						m.refreshChat()
557|					}
558|				} else {
559|					m.appendAgent(aid, ev.Str("role"), ev.Str("text"), false)
560|				}
561|			}
562|			break
563|		}
564|		if i, ok := m.streams[id]; ok && i < len(m.chat) {
565|			m.chat[i].text = ev.Str("text")
566|			m.chat[i].role = ev.Str("role")
567|			delete(m.streams, id)
568|			m.refreshChat()
569|			break
570|		}
571|		if aid == "" {
572|			m.chat = append(m.chat, chatLine{id: id, role: ev.Str("role"), text: ev.Str("text")})
573|			m.refreshChat()
574|		} else {
575|			m.appendAgent(aid, ev.Str("role"), ev.Str("text"), false)
576|		}
577|	case "ChatHistoryAdded":
578|		aid := ev.Str("agent_id")
579|		if aid == "" {
580|			if ev.Int("index") == 0 {
581|				m.chat = m.chat[:0]
582|			}
583|			m.chat = append(m.chat, chatLine{id: ev.Str("id"), role: ev.Str("role"), text: ev.Str("text")})
584|			m.refreshChat()
585|		} else {
586|			m.appendAgent(aid, ev.Str("role"), ev.Str("text"), ev.Int("index") == 0)
587|		}
588|	case "FileContent":
589|		m.openFileTab(ev.Str("path"), ev.Str("content"))
590|	case "FileEdited":
591|		path := ev.Str("path")
592|		diff := ev.Str("diff")
593|		found := false
594|		for i := range m.tabs {
595|			if m.tabs[i].path == path {
596|				m.tabs[i].diff = diff
597|				m.tabs[i].edited = true
598|				found = true
599|			}
600|		}
601|		m.tree.reload()
602|		if !found && path != "" {
603|			return m.send(protocol.OpenFile(path))
604|		}
605|	case "FileTreeUpdated", "PathChanged":
606|		m.tree.reload()
607|		m.status = ev.Type
608|	case "GitStateUpdated":
609|		m.applyGit(ev.Map("git"))
610|		m.refreshInspect()
611|	case "SnapshotReady":
612|		snap := ev.Map("snapshot")
613|		if g, ok := snap["git"].(map[string]any); ok {
614|			m.applyGit(g)
615|		}
616|		if s, ok := snap["stats"].(map[string]any); ok {
617|			m.applyStats(s)
618|		} else {

plan.md  lines 1-39 of 39
 1|# Product TUI
 2|
 3|Go + Charm client for the Codeloom engine. Assumes the engine TUI protocol slice is already merged (`CreatePath`, `RenamePath`, `DeletePath`, `RequestContext`, `RequestMemory`, `RequestAgentTranscript`, full `OpenFile`, `MemoryUpdated`, `ContextBreakdown`, git-on-write).
 4|
 5|`codeloom-tui` hosts the engine: attach if `{workspace}/.engine/engine.sock` already accepts connections, otherwise spawn `CODELOOM_ENGINE` → `engine` on `PATH` → sibling `engine` binary → `python3 app.py`. Owned children get `Shutdown` then SIGTERM/SIGKILL on quit. Attached engines are left running.
 6|
 7|## Layout
 8|
 9|```
10|+-- files (nvim) --+-- [chat] [file...] [agent...] --+-- stats / git ------+
11|| tree             |  default: orch chat             |  $  tokens  branch  |
12|| git glyphs       |  Enter on file -> new tab       |  staged/unstaged/?  |
13|| a A d r Enter    |  click agent -> transcript tab  +-- agent graph ------+
14||                  |  file tab: full buffer,         |  orch               |
15||                  |  ctrl+d toggles diff overlay    |    coder (live)     |
16||                  |                                 +-- context | memory -+
17||                  |                                 |  usage bar + dump   |
18|+------------------+---------------------------------+---------------------+
19|| > message or /command              [abort]                                |
20|```
21|
22|## Keys
23|
24|- `tab` / `ctrl+w` / `shift+tab` — cycle panes
25|- Tree: `hjkl`, `enter` open, `a` file, `A` dir, `r` rename, `d` delete, `R` refresh
26|- Center: `enter` send, `ctrl+t`/`ctrl+p` next/prev tab, `ctrl+e` close tab, `ctrl+d` file diff
27|- Right: `h`/`l` context|memory, `j`/`k` agents, `[`/`]` context sections, `enter` transcript, `r` refresh
28|- Global: `ctrl+x` abort, `ctrl+u` undo, `ctrl+c` quit
29|- Mouse: tabs, git paths, memory files, agent rows
30|
31|## Stack
32|
33|Bubble Tea + Bubbles + Lip Gloss + Glamour + BubbleZone. Protocol types live in `internal/protocol` and round-trip `testdata/protocol/golden.ndjson` copied from engine tests.
34|
35|## Run
36|
37|```
38|go run ./cmd/codeloom-tui [workspace]
39|```

verdict

go build ./..., go vet ./..., go test ./internal/tui/... all pass; gofmt -l clean after running gofmt -w on app.go/themes.go; get_diagnostics clean on all changed files except two pre-existing repo-wide lint hints unrelated to this change (unused sectionBorder in styles.go, user-defined min/max redundant hint in app.go, writestring style hint in the new viewSettings — none are errors).

facts
  • Settings row 0 = theme (left/right/enter cycles through themes.go list, wraps); row 1 = showLineNumbers toggle (enter/space flips it and calls m.refreshFileVP()). Every change calls saveSettingsNow(); esc/s closes and also saves.
  • Theme switching only touches the package color vars via applyTheme; no other file's color-var references were modified.
  • settingsOpen is checked first in handleKey, ahead of the modalKind check, so settings and file/dir modals are mutually exclusive but independent flags — opening one while the other might be open isn't guarded further (task said this is fine/simplest).

215|		m.listen(),

217|		m.send(protocol.RequestSnapshot(true)),
218|		m.send(protocol.RequestGit()),

226|func tick() tea.Cmd {
227|	return tea.Tick(4*time.Second, func(t time.Time) tea.Msg { return tickMsg(t) })
228|}

230|func (m Model) listen() tea.Cmd {
231|	if m.events == nil {
232|		return nil
233|	}
234|	ch := m.events
235|	return func() tea.Msg {
236|		ev, ok := <-ch
237|		if !ok {
238|			return errMsg{fmt.Errorf("engine disconnected")}
239|		}
240|		return eventMsg(ev)
241|	}
242|}
243|
244|func (m Model) send(cmd protocol.Command) tea.Cmd {
245|	return func() tea.Msg {
246|		if m.conn == nil {
247|			return errMsg{fmt.Errorf("not connected")}
248|		}
249|		if err := m.conn.Send(cmd); err != nil {
250|			return errMsg{err}
251|		}
252|		return nil
253|	}
254|}

256|func (m Model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
257|	var cmds []tea.Cmd
258|	switch msg := msg.(type) {
259|	case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
260|		m.width, m.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
261|		m.layoutViewports()
262|	case tickMsg:
263|		cmds = append(cmds, tick())
264|	case spinner.TickMsg:
265|		var cmd tea.Cmd
266|		m.spin, cmd = m.spin.Update(msg)
267|		if m.busy() {
268|			cmds = append(cmds, cmd)
269|			m.refreshGraph()
270|		}
271|	case errMsg:
272|		if msg.err != nil {
273|			m.lastErr = msg.err.Error()
274|		}
275|		cmds = append(cmds, m.listen())
276|	case eventMsg:
277|		if cmd := m.handleEvent(protocol.Event(msg)); cmd != nil {
278|			cmds = append(cmds, cmd)
279|		}
280|		cmds = append(cmds, m.listen())
281|		if m.busy() {
282|			cmds = append(cmds, m.spin.Tick)
283|		}
284|	case tea.MouseMsg:
285|		if cmd := m.handleMouse(msg); cmd != nil {
286|			cmds = append(cmds, cmd)
287|		}
288|	case tea.KeyMsg:
289|		cmd, handled := m.handleKey(msg)
290|		if cmd != nil {
291|			cmds = append(cmds, cmd)
292|		}
293|		if handled {
294|			return m, tea.Batch(cmds...)
295|		}
296|		var c tea.Cmd
297|		switch {
298|		case m.modal != modalNone:
299|			m.modalInput, c = m.modalInput.Update(msg)
300|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
301|		case m.focus == focusComposer:
302|			m.input, c = m.input.Update(msg)
303|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
304|			m.layoutViewports()
305|		case m.focus == focusCode:
306|			m.fileVP, c = m.fileVP.Update(msg)
307|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
308|		case m.focus == focusChat:
309|			m.chatVP, c = m.chatVP.Update(msg)
310|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
311|			m.chatFollow = m.chatVP.AtBottom()
312|		case m.focus == focusGraph:
313|			m.graphVP, c = m.graphVP.Update(msg)
314|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
315|		case m.focus == focusInspect:
316|			m.inspectVP, c = m.inspectVP.Update(msg)
317|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
318|		}
319|		return m, tea.Batch(cmds...)
320|	}
321|	return m, tea.Batch(cmds...)
322|}

247|func (m *Model) handlePromptKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) (tea.Cmd, bool) {
248|	if m.promptID == "" {
249|		return nil, false
250|	}
251|	if m.focus != focusChat && m.focus != focusComposer {
252|		return nil, false
253|	}
254|	k := m.keys
255|	empty := strings.TrimSpace(m.input.Value()) == ""
256|	switch {
257|	case key.Matches(msg, k.Escape):
258|		if m.focus == focusComposer && !empty {
259|			m.input.Reset()
260|			return nil, true
261|		}
262|		return m.answerPrompt(m.promptDecline()), true
263|	case msg.String() == "enter":
264|		if !empty {
265|			return m.submitComposer(), true
266|		}
267|		if len(m.promptChoices) == 0 {
268|			return nil, true
269|		}
270|		return m.answerPrompt(m.selectedPromptChoice()), true
271|	case msg.String() == "up" || (empty && msg.String() == "k"):
272|		m.movePromptChoice(-1)
273|		return nil, true
274|	case msg.String() == "down" || (empty && msg.String() == "j"):
275|		m.movePromptChoice(1)
276|		return nil, true
277|	}
278|	if empty && len(m.promptChoices) > 0 {
279|		s := msg.String()
280|		if len(s) == 1 && s[0] >= '1' && s[0] <= '9' {
281|			idx := int(s[0] - '1')
282|			if idx < len(m.promptChoices) {
283|				return m.answerPrompt(m.promptChoices[idx]), true
284|			}
285|		}
286|	}
287|	return nil, false
288|}

304|			t.showDiff = !t.showDiff
305|			m.refreshFileVP()
306|		}
307|		return nil, true
308|	}
309|	return nil, false

328|	case msg.String() == "k", msg.String() == "up":
329|		m.moveGraphCursor(-1)
330|		return nil, true
331|	}
332|	return nil, false

 12|	left, center, right, body int
 13|	compact, hideInspect      bool

 15|
 16|func (m Model) layout() layout {
 17|	w, h := m.width, m.height
 18|	if w < 1 {
 19|		w = 80
 20|	}
 21|	if h < 1 {
 22|		h = 24
 23|	}
 24|	d := layout{body: max(8, h-1)}
 25|	switch {
 26|	case w < 90:
 27|		d.compact = true
 28|		d.hideInspect = true
 29|		d.center = w
 30|	case w < 120:
 31|		d.left, d.right = 24, 32
 32|		d.center = max(20, w-d.left-d.right)
 33|	default:
 34|		d.left, d.right = 28, 36
 35|		d.center = max(20, w-d.left-d.right)
 36|	}
 37|	return d
 38|}

 55|	compH := m.composerHeight()

 61|

 64|		rightW = min(36, max(24, m.width/2))
 65|	}

 86|func (m Model) View() string {
 87|	if m.width == 0 {
 88|		return "starting…"
 89|	}

plan.md  lines 1-39 of 39
 1|# Product TUI
 2|
 3|Go + Charm client for the Codeloom engine. Assumes the engine TUI protocol slice is already merged (`CreatePath`, `RenamePath`, `DeletePath`, `RequestContext`, `RequestMemory`, `RequestAgentTranscript`, full `OpenFile`, `MemoryUpdated`, `ContextBreakdown`, git-on-write).
 4|
 5|`codeloom-tui` hosts the engine: attach if `{workspace}/.engine/engine.sock` already accepts connections, otherwise spawn `CODELOOM_ENGINE` → `engine` on `PATH` → sibling `engine` binary → `python3 app.py`. Owned children get `Shutdown` then SIGTERM/SIGKILL on quit. Attached engines are left running.
 6|
 7|## Layout
 8|
 9|```
10|+-- files (nvim) --+-- [chat] [file...] [agent...] --+-- stats / git ------+
11|| tree             |  default: orch chat             |  $  tokens  branch  |
12|| git glyphs       |  Enter on file -> new tab       |  staged/unstaged/?  |
13|| a A d r Enter    |  click agent -> transcript tab  +-- agent graph ------+
14||                  |  file tab: full buffer,         |  orch               |
15||                  |  ctrl+d toggles diff overlay    |    coder (live)     |
16||                  |                                 +-- context | memory -+
17||                  |                                 |  usage bar + dump   |
18|+------------------+---------------------------------+---------------------+
19|| > message or /command              [abort]                                |
20|```
21|
22|## Keys
23|
24|- `tab` / `ctrl+w` / `shift+tab` — cycle panes
25|- Tree: `hjkl`, `enter` open, `a` file, `A` dir, `r` rename, `d` delete, `R` refresh
26|- Center: `enter` send, `ctrl+t`/`ctrl+p` next/prev tab, `ctrl+e` close tab, `ctrl+d` file diff
27|- Right: `h`/`l` context|memory, `j`/`k` agents, `[`/`]` context sections, `enter` transcript, `r` refresh
28|- Global: `ctrl+x` abort, `ctrl+u` undo, `ctrl+c` quit
29|- Mouse: tabs, git paths, memory files, agent rows
30|
31|## Stack
32|
33|Bubble Tea + Bubbles + Lip Gloss + Glamour + BubbleZone. Protocol types live in `internal/protocol` and round-trip `testdata/protocol/golden.ndjson` copied from engine tests.
34|
35|## Run
36|
37|```
38|go run ./cmd/codeloom-tui [workspace]
39|```

30|func renderSource(path, content string) string {
31|	highlighted, ok := highlight(path, content)
32|	if !ok {
33|		highlighted = content
34|	}
35|	return withLineNumbers(highlighted)

37|
38|func highlight(path, content string) (string, bool) {
39|	if content == "" {
40|		return "", false
41|	}
42|	lexer := lexers.Match(filepath.Base(path))
43|	if lexer == nil {
44|		lexer = lexers.Analyse(content)
45|	}
46|	if lexer == nil {
47|		lexer = lexers.Fallback
48|	}
49|	lexer = chroma.Coalesce(lexer)
50|	it, err := lexer.Tokenise(nil, content)
51|	if err != nil {
52|		return "", false
53|	}
54|	style := styles.Get("tokyonight-night")
55|	if style == nil {
56|		style = styles.Fallback
57|	}
58|	var buf bytes.Buffer
59|	if err := formatters.TTY16m.Format(&buf, style, it); err != nil {
60|		return "", false
61|	}
62|	return buf.String(), true
63|}
64|
65|func withLineNumbers(content string) string {
66|	content = strings.TrimSuffix(content, "\n")
67|	lines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
68|	var b strings.Builder
69|	gutter := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(dim)
70|	for i, line := range lines {
71|		b.WriteString(gutter.Render(fmt.Sprintf("%4d │ ", i+1)))
72|		b.WriteString(line)
73|		b.WriteByte('\n')
74|	}
75|	return b.String()
76|}
77|
78|func renderDiff(content string) string {
79|	add := gitAdd()
80|	del := gitUntracked()
81|	hunk := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(accent)
82|	gutter := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(dim)
83|	var b strings.Builder
84|	for i, line := range strings.Split(content, "\n") {
85|		b.WriteString(gutter.Render(fmt.Sprintf("%4d │ ", i+1)))
86|		switch {
87|		case strings.HasPrefix(line, "+") && !strings.HasPrefix(line, "+++"):
88|			b.WriteString(add.Render(line))
89|		case strings.HasPrefix(line, "-") && !strings.HasPrefix(line, "---"):
90|			b.WriteString(del.Render(line))
91|		case strings.HasPrefix(line, "@@"):
92|			b.WriteString(hunk.Render(line))
93|		default:
94|			b.WriteString(line)
95|		}
96|		b.WriteByte('\n')
97|	}
98|	return b.String()
99|}

+// applyTheme copies the given theme's colors into the package-level color
+// vars above so every existing call site (chat.go, tabs.go, graph.go,
+// layout.go, context.go, fileview.go, stats.go, app.go, ...) picks up the
+// change without needing to be touched.
+func applyTheme(t theme) {
+	bg = t.bg
+	bgAlt = t.bgAlt
+	fg = t.fg
+	dim = t.dim
+	accent = t.accent
+	green = t.green
+	red = t.red
+	yellow = t.yellow
+	magenta = t.magenta
+	border = t.border
+	focusC = t.focusC
+}

+func themeNames() []string {

+func themeIndex(name string) int {

+		if t.name == name {
+			return i
+		}
+	}
+	return -1
+}
+
+// setThemeByIndex activates the theme at idx (wrapping into range) and
+// applies it to the package-level color vars.
+func setThemeByIndex(idx int) {
+	n := len(themes)
+	if n == 0 {
+		return
+	}
+	idx = ((idx % n) + n) % n
+	currentTheme = themes[idx]
+	applyTheme(currentTheme)
+}
+
+// setTheme activates the named theme, falling back to Tokyo Night if the
+// name is unknown.
+func setTheme(name string) {
+	if idx := themeIndex(name); idx >= 0 {
+		setThemeByIndex(idx)
+		return
+	}
+	setThemeByIndex(0)
+}

@@ -1,0 +1,65 @@
+package tui
+
+import (
+	"encoding/json"
+	"os"
+	"path/filepath"
+)
+
+// persistedSettings is the on-disk JSON shape for user TUI preferences.
+type persistedSettings struct {
+	Theme           string `json:"theme"`
+	ShowLineNumbers bool   `json:"show_line_numbers"`
+}
+
+func settingsPath() (string, error) {
+	dir, err := os.UserConfigDir()
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	return filepath.Join(dir, "codeloom-tui", "settings.json"), nil
+}
+
+// loadSettings reads persisted settings from disk. Callers should fall
+// back to defaults silently if it returns an error.
+func loadSettings() (persistedSettings, error) {
+	var s persistedSettings
+	path, err := settingsPath()
+	if err != nil {
+		return s, err
+	}
+	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return s, err
+	}
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &s); err != nil {
+		return s, err
+	}
+	return s, nil
+}
+
+// saveSettings writes settings to disk, creating the config directory if
+// needed. Errors are returned for the caller to ignore/log; they must not
+// crash the UI.
+func saveSettings(s persistedSettings) error {
+	path, err := settingsPath()
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o755); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(s, "", "  ")
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return os.WriteFile(path, data, 0o644)
+}
+
+// currentSettings snapshots the model's current settings for persisting.
+func (m Model) currentSettings() persistedSettings {
+	return persistedSettings{
+		Theme:           currentTheme.name,
+		ShowLineNumbers: m.showLineNumbers,
+	}
+}

 	}
 	m.setFocus(m.paneAt(msg))

+
+// settingsRowCount is the number of navigable rows in the settings modal:
+// the theme picker plus each toggle option.
+const settingsRowCount = 2
+
+func (m *Model) openSettings() {
+	m.settingsOpen = true
+	m.settingsCursor = 0
+	m.settingsThemeIdx = themeIndex(currentTheme.name)
+	if m.settingsThemeIdx < 0 {
+		m.settingsThemeIdx = 0
+	}
+}
+
+func (m *Model) closeSettings() {
+	m.settingsOpen = false
+	if err := saveSettings(m.currentSettings()); err != nil {
+		m.lastErr = "settings save: " + err.Error()
+	}
+}
+
+func (m *Model) handleSettingsKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) tea.Cmd {
+	switch msg.String() {
+	case "esc", "s":
+		m.closeSettings()
+		return nil
+	case "up", "k":
+		m.settingsCursor--
+		if m.settingsCursor < 0 {
+			m.settingsCursor = settingsRowCount - 1
+		}
+		return nil
+	case "down", "j":
+		m.settingsCursor++
+		if m.settingsCursor >= settingsRowCount {
+			m.settingsCursor = 0
+		}
+		return nil
+	case "left", "h":
+		if m.settingsCursor == 0 {
+			setThemeByIndex(m.settingsThemeIdx - 1)
+			m.settingsThemeIdx = themeIndex(currentTheme.name)
+			m.saveSettingsNow()
+		}
+		return nil
+	case "right", "l":
+		if m.settingsCursor == 0 {
+			setThemeByIndex(m.settingsThemeIdx + 1)
+			m.settingsThemeIdx = themeIndex(currentTheme.name)
+			m.saveSettingsNow()
+		}
+		return nil
+	case "enter", " ":
+		switch m.settingsCursor {
+		case 0:
+			setThemeByIndex(m.settingsThemeIdx + 1)
+			m.settingsThemeIdx = themeIndex(currentTheme.name)
+		case 1:
+			m.showLineNumbers = !m.showLineNumbers
+			m.refreshFileVP()
+		}
+		m.saveSettingsNow()
+		return nil
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (m *Model) saveSettingsNow() {
+	if err := saveSettings(m.currentSettings()); err != nil {
+		m.lastErr = "settings save: " + err.Error()
+	}
 }

+func (m Model) viewSettings() string {
+	var b strings.Builder
+	b.WriteString(titleStyle().Render("settings") + "\n\n")
+
+	themeRow := "Theme:  " + themeRowLabel(m.settingsThemeIdx)
+	if m.settingsCursor == 0 {
+		b.WriteString(lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(accent).Bold(true).Render("❯ " + themeRow))
+	} else {
+		b.WriteString(dimStyle().Render("  " + themeRow))
+	}
+	b.WriteString("\n")
+
+	linesRow := "Line numbers:  " + checkbox(m.showLineNumbers)
+	if m.settingsCursor == 1 {
+		b.WriteString(lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(accent).Bold(true).Render("❯ " + linesRow))
+	} else {
+		b.WriteString(dimStyle().Render("  " + linesRow))
+	}
+	b.WriteString("\n\n")
+
+	b.WriteString(dimStyle().Render("↑/↓ navigate · enter/space toggle · ←/→ theme · esc close"))
+	return lipgloss.NewStyle().
+		Border(lipgloss.RoundedBorder()).
+		BorderForeground(accent).
+		Padding(1, 2).
+		Width(min(60, max(24, m.width-4))).
+		Render(strings.TrimRight(b.String(), "\n"))
+}
+
+func themeRowLabel(idx int) string {
+	names := themeNames()
+	if idx < 0 || idx >= len(names) {
+		return "< ? >"
+	}
+	return "< " + names[idx] + " >"
+}
+
+func checkbox(on bool) string {
+	if on {
+		return "[x]"
+	}
+	return "[ ]"
+}

+// settingsRowCount is the number of navigable rows in the settings modal:
+// the theme picker plus each toggle option.
+const settingsRowCount = 2
+
+func (m *Model) openSettings() {
+	m.settingsOpen = true
+	m.settingsCursor = 0
+	m.settingsThemeIdx = themeIndex(currentTheme.name)
+	if m.settingsThemeIdx < 0 {
+		m.settingsThemeIdx = 0
+	}
+}
+
+func (m *Model) closeSettings() {
+	m.settingsOpen = false
+	m.saveSettingsNow()
+}
+
+func (m *Model) handleSettingsKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) tea.Cmd {
+	switch msg.String() {
+	case "esc", "s":
+		m.closeSettings()
+		return nil
+	case "up", "k":
+		m.settingsCursor--
+		if m.settingsCursor < 0 {
+			m.settingsCursor = settingsRowCount - 1
+		}
+		return nil
+	case "down", "j":
+		m.settingsCursor++
+		if m.settingsCursor >= settingsRowCount {
+			m.settingsCursor = 0
+		}
+		return nil
+	case "left", "h":
+		if m.settingsCursor == 0 {
+			setThemeByIndex(m.settingsThemeIdx - 1)
+			m.settingsThemeIdx = themeIndex(currentTheme.

 74|// currentTheme is the active palette. It starts as Tokyo Night and is
 75|// reassigned (and mirrored into the package color vars via applyTheme) by
 76|// setTheme / setThemeByIndex.
 77|var currentTheme = themes[0]
 78|
 79|func themeNames() []string {
 80|	names := make([]string, len(themes))
 81|	for i, t := range themes {
 82|		names[i] = t.name
 83|	}
 84|	return names
 85|}
 86|
 87|func themeIndex(name string) int {
 88|	for i, t := range themes {
 89|		if t.name == name {
 90|			return i
 91|		}
 92|	}
 93|	return -1
 94|}
 95|
 96|// setThemeByIndex activates the theme at idx (wrapping into range) and
 97|// applies it to the package-level color vars.
 98|func setThemeByIndex(idx int) {
 99|	n := len(themes)
100|	if n == 0 {
101|		return
102|	}
103|	idx = ((idx % n) + n) % n
104|	currentTheme = themes[idx]
105|	applyTheme(currentTheme)
106|}
107|
108|// setTheme activates the named theme, falling back to Tokyo Night if the
109|// name is unknown.
110|func setTheme(name string) {
111|	if idx := themeIndex(name); idx >= 0 {
112|		setThemeByIndex(idx)
113|		return
114|	}
115|	setThemeByIndex(0)
116|}

internal/tui/settings_persist.go  lines 1-65 of 65
 1|package tui
 2|
 3|import (
 4|	"encoding/json"
 5|	"os"
 6|	"path/filepath"
 7|)
 8|
 9|// persistedSettings is the on-disk JSON shape for user TUI preferences.
10|type persistedSettings struct {
11|	Theme           string `json:"theme"`
12|	ShowLineNumbers bool   `json:"show_line_numbers"`
13|}
14|
15|func settingsPath() (string, error) {
16|	dir, err := os.UserConfigDir()
17|	if err != nil {
18|		return "", err
19|	}
20|	return filepath.Join(dir, "codeloom-tui", "settings.json"), nil
21|}
22|
23|// loadSettings reads persisted settings from disk. Callers should fall
24|// back to defaults silently if it returns an error.
25|func loadSettings() (persistedSettings, error) {
26|	var s persistedSettings
27|	path, err := settingsPath()
28|	if err != nil {
29|		return s, err
30|	}
31|	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
32|	if err != nil {
33|		return s, err
34|	}
35|	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &s); err != nil {
36|		return s, err
37|	}
38|	return s, nil
39|}
40|
41|// saveSettings writes settings to disk, creating the config directory if
42|// needed. Errors are returned for the caller to ignore/log; they must not
43|// crash the UI.
44|func saveSettings(s persistedSettings) error {
45|	path, err := settingsPath()
46|	if err != nil {
47|		return err
48|	}
49|	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o755); err != nil {
50|		return err
51|	}
52|	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(s, "", "  ")
53|	if err != nil {
54|		return err
55|	}
56|	return os.WriteFile(path, data, 0o644)
57|}
58|
59|// currentSettings snapshots the model's current settings for persisting.
60|func (m Model) currentSettings() persistedSettings {
61|	return persistedSettings{
62|		Theme:           currentTheme.name,
63|		ShowLineNumbers: m.showLineNumbers,
64|	}
65|}

orchestratorI've kicked off a survey of the codebase to understand the TUI framework, current theming setup, config/persistence, and any existing modal patterns. I'll report back once that comes in, then plan the settings modal imp…

I've kicked off a survey of the codebase to understand the TUI framework, current theming setup, config/persistence, and any existing modal patterns. I'll report back once that comes in, then plan the settings modal implementation with the coder.

ok ask 8b37511e $0.311 · 554,142 tok

I now have all the pieces to write a thorough briefing.

verdict

Adding a settings modal fits cleanly into the existing single-Model/modal architecture. Minimum required changes: (1) new modalKind (or standalone bool+struct) for settings state (selected tab/section, theme cursor, toggle values); (2) new key.Binding + handleKey case to open it; (3) key handler for navigating/committing the settings modal (list nav + toggle + save); (4) new view function to render it, invoked from View() guarded by the new modal state, using overlay(); (5) refactor styles.go's hardcoded lipgloss.Color vars into a swappable theme structure (e.g. type theme struct{bg,bgAlt,fg,dim,accent,green,red,yellow,magenta,border,focusC lipgloss.Color} with a package var currentTheme and either keep the same-named exported accessor vars recomputed on theme change, or convert every color reference to currentTheme.X — the former requires re-pointing the vars, doable since Go allows reassigning package vars at runtime, e.g. bg = newTheme.bg etc. inside an applyTheme(name string) func); also update glamour style at chat.go:259 to follow the theme (light/dark). (6) add a persistence file (new — none exists) e.g. JSON under os.UserConfigDir()/codeloom/settings.json, loaded in main.go before tui.New(...) or inside tui.New itself, and saved on modal commit.

leftover

No existing config/settings infrastructure was found to build on — this must be created new; confirm with the team where user preferences should live (per-workspace .engine/ dir alongside the socket vs global ~/.config/codeloom/) since host.go already establishes a per-workspace .engine/ directory convention that could double as a place for a settings file, or a global XDG config dir might be preferred for cross-project theme choice — I did not find any prior-art decision on this in plan.md or elsewhere, so this is a product decision for the requester rather than a codebase gap.

14|func main() {
15|	workspace := "."
16|	if len(os.Args) > 1 {
17|		workspace = os.Args[1]
18|	}
19|	workspace, err := filepath.Abs(workspace)
20|	if err != nil {
21|		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "workspace: %v\n", err)
22|		os.Exit(1)
23|	}
24|	eng, err := host.AttachOrSpawn(workspace)
25|	if err != nil {
26|		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "engine: %v\n", err)
27|		os.Exit(1)
28|	}
29|	conn, err := client.Dial(eng.Sock)
30|	if err != nil {
31|		eng.Kill()
32|		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "connect: %v\n", err)
33|		os.Exit(1)
34|	}
35|	model := tui.New(workspace, eng, conn)
36|	p := tea.NewProgram(model, tea.WithAltScreen(), tea.WithMouseCellMotion())
37|	final, err := p.Run()
38|	if m, ok := final.(tui.Model); ok {
39|		m.Close()
40|	} else {
41|		_ = conn.Close()
42|		eng.Kill()
43|	}
44|	if err != nil {
45|		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "tui: %v\n", err)
46|		os.Exit(1)
47|	}

213|func (m Model) Init() tea.Cmd {
214|	return tea.Batch(
215|		m.listen(),
216|		m.send(protocol.StartSession(m.workspace, "")),
217|		m.send(protocol.RequestSnapshot(true)),
218|		m.send(protocol.RequestGit()),
219|		m.send(protocol.RequestMemory()),
220|		m.send(protocol.RequestContext("")),
221|		m.spin.Tick,
222|		tick(),
223|	)
224|}
225|
226|func tick() tea.Cmd {
227|	return tea.Tick(4*time.Second, func(t time.Time) tea.Msg { return tickMsg(t) })
228|}
229|
230|func (m Model) listen() tea.Cmd {
231|	if m.events == nil {
232|		return nil
233|	}
234|	ch := m.events
235|	return func() tea.Msg {
236|		ev, ok := <-ch
237|		if !ok {
238|			return errMsg{fmt.Errorf("engine disconnected")}
239|		}
240|		return eventMsg(ev)
241|	}
242|}
243|
244|func (m Model) send(cmd protocol.Command) tea.Cmd {
245|	return func() tea.Msg {
246|		if m.conn == nil {
247|			return errMsg{fmt.Errorf("not connected")}
248|		}
249|		if err := m.conn.Send(cmd); err != nil {
250|			return errMsg{err}
251|		}
252|		return nil
253|	}
254|}

247|func (m *Model) handlePromptKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) (tea.Cmd, bool) {
248|	if m.promptID == "" {
249|		return nil, false
250|	}
251|	if m.focus != focusChat && m.focus != focusComposer {
252|		return nil, false
253|	}
254|	k := m.keys
255|	empty := strings.TrimSpace(m.input.Value()) == ""
256|	switch {
257|	case key.Matches(msg, k.Escape):
258|		if m.focus == focusComposer && !empty {
259|			m.input.Reset()

 20|func SocketPath(workspace string) string {
 21|	return filepath.Join(workspace, ".engine", "engine.sock")

 23|
 24|func AttachOrSpawn(workspace string) (*Engine, error) {
 25|	ws, err := filepath.Abs(workspace)
 26|	if err != nil {
 27|		return nil, err
 28|	}
 29|	sock := SocketPath(ws)
 30|	if canDial(sock) {
 31|		return &Engine{Sock: sock, Workspace: ws, Owned: false}, nil
 32|	}
 33|	bin, args, dir := LookupEngine(ws)
 34|	if bin == "" {
 35|		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no engine binary or python app.py found; start the engine or set CODELOOM_ENGINE")
 36|	}
 37|	cmd := exec.Command(bin, args...)
 38|	cmd.Dir = dir
 39|	cmd.Env = os.Environ()
 40|	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{Setpgid: true}
 41|	cmd.Stdout = os.Stderr
 42|	cmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
 43|	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
 44|		return nil, fmt.Errorf("spawn engine: %w", err)
 45|	}
 46|	eng := &Engine{Cmd: cmd, Owned: true, Sock: sock, Workspace: ws}
 47|	if err := waitSock(sock, 8*time.Second); err != nil {
 48|		eng.Kill()
 49|		return nil, err
 50|	}
 51|	return eng, nil
 52|}
 53|
 54|func LookupEngine(workspace string) (bin string, args []string, dir string) {
 55|	if env := os.Getenv("CODELOOM_ENGINE"); env != "" {
 56|		return env, []string{workspace}, filepath.Dir(env)
 57|	}
 58|	if p, err := exec.LookPath("engine"); err == nil {
 59|		return p, []string{workspace}, workspace
 60|	}

837|func num(v any) float64 {
838|	switch t := v.(type) {
839|	case float64:
840|		return t
841|	case float32:
842|		return float64(t)
843|	case int:
844|		return float64(t)
845|	case int64:
846|		return float64(t)
847|	case int32:
848|		return float64(t)
849|	case string:
850|		n, _ := strconv.ParseFloat(strings.TrimPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(t), "$"), 64)
851|		return n
852|	}
853|	return 0

plan.md  lines 1-39 of 39
 1|# Product TUI
 2|
 3|Go + Charm client for the Codeloom engine. Assumes the engine TUI protocol slice is already merged (`CreatePath`, `RenamePath`, `DeletePath`, `RequestContext`, `RequestMemory`, `RequestAgentTranscript`, full `OpenFile`, `MemoryUpdated`, `ContextBreakdown`, git-on-write).
 4|
 5|`codeloom-tui` hosts the engine: attach if `{workspace}/.engine/engine.sock` already accepts connections, otherwise spawn `CODELOOM_ENGINE` → `engine` on `PATH` → sibling `engine` binary → `python3 app.py`. Owned children get `Shutdown` then SIGTERM/SIGKILL on quit. Attached engines are left running.
 6|
 7|## Layout
 8|
 9|```
10|+-- files (nvim) --+-- [chat] [file...] [agent...] --+-- stats / git ------+
11|| tree             |  default: orch chat             |  $  tokens  branch  |
12|| git glyphs       |  Enter on file -> new tab       |  staged/unstaged/?  |
13|| a A d r Enter    |  click agent -> transcript tab  +-- agent graph ------+
14||                  |  file tab: full buffer,         |  orch               |
15||                  |  ctrl+d toggles diff overlay    |    coder (live)     |
16||                  |                                 +-- context | memory -+
17||                  |                                 |  usage bar + dump   |
18|+------------------+---------------------------------+---------------------+
19|| > message or /command              [abort]                                |
20|```
21|
22|## Keys
23|
24|- `tab` / `ctrl+w` / `shift+tab` — cycle panes
25|- Tree: `hjkl`, `enter` open, `a` file, `A` dir, `r` rename, `d` delete, `R` refresh
26|- Center: `enter` send, `ctrl+t`/`ctrl+p` next/prev tab, `ctrl+e` close tab, `ctrl+d` file diff
27|- Right: `h`/`l` context|memory, `j`/`k` agents, `[`/`]` context sections, `enter` transcript, `r` refresh
28|- Global: `ctrl+x` abort, `ctrl+u` undo, `ctrl+c` quit
29|- Mouse: tabs, git paths, memory files, agent rows
30|
31|## Stack
32|
33|Bubble Tea + Bubbles + Lip Gloss + Glamour + BubbleZone. Protocol types live in `internal/protocol` and round-trip `testdata/protocol/golden.ndjson` copied from engine tests.
34|
35|## Run
36|
37|```
38|go run ./cmd/codeloom-tui [workspace]
39|```

146|func (m *Model) movePromptChoice(delta int) {
147|	n := len(m.promptChoices)
148|	if n == 0 {
149|		return
150|	}
151|	m.promptCursor += delta
152|	if m.promptCursor < 0 {
153|		m.promptCursor = n - 1
154|	}
155|	if m.promptCursor >= n {
156|		m.promptCursor = 0
157|	}
158|}
159|
160|func (m Model) selectedPromptChoice() string {
161|	if m.promptCursor >= 0 && m.promptCursor < len(m.promptChoices) {
162|		return m.promptChoices[m.promptCursor]
163|	}
164|	return ""
165|}
166|
167|func (m Model) promptDecline() string {
168|	for _, want := range []string{"deny", "no", "cancel", "reject", "skip", "never"} {
169|		for _, c := range m.promptChoices {
170|			if strings.EqualFold(c, want) {
171|				return c
172|			}
173|		}
174|	}
175|	if n := len(m.promptChoices); n > 0 {
176|		return m.promptChoices[n-1]
177|	}
178|	return ""

18|	}
19|	workspace, err := filepath.Abs(workspace)

22|		os.Exit(1)
23|	}
24|	eng, err := host.AttachOrSpawn(workspace)

28|	}

247|func (m *Model) handlePromptKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) (tea.Cmd, bool) {
248|	if m.promptID == "" {
249|		return nil, false
250|	}
251|	if m.focus != focusChat && m.focus != focusComposer {
252|		return nil, false
253|	}
254|	k := m.keys
255|	empty := strings.TrimSpace(m.input.Value()) == ""
256|	switch {
257|	case key.Matches(msg, k.Escape):
258|		if m.focus == focusComposer && !empty {
259|			m.input.Reset()
260|			return nil, true
261|		}
262|		return m.answerPrompt(m.promptDecline()), true
263|	case msg.String() == "enter":
264|		if !empty {
265|			return m.submitComposer(), true
266|		}
267|		if len(m.promptChoices) == 0 {
268|			return nil, true
269|		}
270|		return m.answerPrompt(m.selectedPromptChoice()), true
271|	case msg.String() == "up" || (empty && msg.String() == "k"):
272|		m.movePromptChoice(-1)
273|		return nil, true
274|	case msg.String() == "down" || (empty && msg.String() == "j"):
275|		m.movePromptChoice(1)
276|		return nil, true
277|	}
278|	if empty && len(m.promptChoices) > 0 {
279|		s := msg.String()
280|		if len(s) == 1 && s[0] >= '1' && s[0] <= '9' {
281|			idx := int(s[0] - '1')
282|			if idx < len(m.promptChoices) {
283|				return m.answerPrompt(m.promptChoices[idx]), true
284|			}
285|		}
286|	}
287|	return nil, false
288|}

213|func (m Model) Init() tea.Cmd {
214|	return tea.Batch(
215|		m.listen(),
216|		m.send(protocol.StartSession(m.workspace, "")),
217|		m.send(protocol.RequestSnapshot(true)),
218|		m.send(protocol.RequestGit()),

226|func tick() tea.Cmd {
227|	return tea.Tick(4*time.Second, func(t time.Time) tea.Msg { return tickMsg(t) })

230|func (m Model) listen() tea.Cmd {
231|	if m.events == nil {
232|		return nil
233|	}
234|	ch := m.events
235|	return func() tea.Msg {
236|		ev, ok := <-ch
237|		if !ok {
238|			return errMsg{fmt.Errorf("engine disconnected")}
239|		}
240|		return eventMsg(ev)
241|	}
242|}
243|
244|func (m Model) send(cmd protocol.Command) tea.Cmd {
245|	return func() tea.Msg {
246|		if m.conn == nil {
247|			return errMsg{fmt.Errorf("not connected")}
248|		}
249|		if err := m.conn.Send(cmd); err != nil {
250|			return errMsg{err}
251|		}
252|		return nil
253|	}
254|}

256|func (m Model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
257|	var cmds []tea.Cmd
258|	switch msg := msg.(type) {
259|	case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
260|		m.width, m.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
261|		m.layoutViewports()
262|	case tickMsg:
263|		cmds = append(cmds, tick())
264|	case spinner.TickMsg:
265|		var cmd tea.Cmd
266|		m.spin, cmd = m.spin.Update(msg)
267|		if m.busy() {
268|			cmds = append(cmds, cmd)
269|			m.refreshGraph()
270|		}
271|	case errMsg:
272|		if msg.err != nil {
273|			m.lastErr = msg.err.Error()
274|		}
275|		cmds = append(cmds, m.listen())
276|	case eventMsg:
277|		if cmd := m.handleEvent(protocol.Event(msg)); cmd != nil {
278|			cmds = append(cmds, cmd)
279|		}
280|		cmds = append(cmds, m.listen())
281|		if m.busy() {
282|			cmds = append(cmds, m.spin.Tick)
283|		}
284|	case tea.MouseMsg:
285|		if cmd := m.handleMouse(msg); cmd != nil {
286|			cmds = append(cmds, cmd)
287|		}
288|	case tea.KeyMsg:
289|		cmd, handled := m.handleKey(msg)
290|		if cmd != nil {
291|			cmds = append(cmds, cmd)
292|		}
293|		if handled {
294|			return m, tea.Batch(cmds...)
295|		}
296|		var c tea.Cmd
297|		switch {
298|		case m.modal != modalNone:
299|			m.modalInput, c = m.modalInput.Update(msg)
300|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
301|		case m.focus == focusComposer:
302|			m.input, c = m.input.Update(msg)
303|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
304|			m.layoutViewports()
305|		case m.focus == focusCode:
306|			m.fileVP, c = m.fileVP.Update(msg)
307|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
308|		case m.focus == focusChat:
309|			m.chatVP, c = m.chatVP.Update(msg)
310|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
311|			m.chatFollow = m.chatVP.AtBottom()
312|		case m.focus == focusGraph:
313|			m.graphVP, c = m.graphVP.Update(msg)
314|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
315|		case m.focus == focusInspect:
316|			m.inspectVP, c = m.inspectVP.Update(msg)
317|			cmds = append(cmds, c)
318|		}
319|		return m, tea.Batch(cmds...)
320|	}
321|	return m, tea.Batch(cmds...)
322|}

257|func renderMarkdown(text string, w int) string {
258|	r, err := glamour.NewTermRenderer(

@@ -1,0 +1,65 @@
+package settings
+
+import (
+	"encoding/json"
+	"os"
+	"path/filepath"
+)
+
+// Settings holds user-tunable TUI preferences that persist across runs.
+type Settings struct {
+	Theme        string `json:"theme"`
+	MouseEnabled bool   `json:"mouseEnabled"`
+}
+
+// Default returns the settings used when none are stored yet.
+func Default() Settings {
+	return Settings{Theme: "tokyo-night", MouseEnabled: true}
+}
+
+// Path returns the on-disk location of the settings file, creating its
+// parent directory if necessary.
+func Path() (string, error) {
+	dir, err := os.UserConfigDir()
+	if err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	dir = filepath.Join(dir, "codeloom")
+	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
+		return "", err
+	}
+	return filepath.Join(dir, "settings.json"), nil
+}
+
+// Load reads settings from disk. Any problem reading or parsing the file
+// (missing file, corrupt JSON, permission issues) falls back to Default()
+// without surfacing an error, since settings are not correctness-critical
+// and should never block TUI startup.
+func Load() (Settings, error) {
+	path, err := Path()
+	if err != nil {
+		return Default(), nil
+	}
+	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
+	if err != nil {
+		return Default(), nil
+	}
+	var s Settings
+	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &s); err != nil {
+		return Default(), nil
+	}
+	return s, nil
+}
+
+// Save writes settings to disk as indented JSON.
+func Save(s Settings) error {
+	path, err := Path()
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	data, err := json.MarshalIndent(s, "", "  ")
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return os.WriteFile(path, data, 0o644)
+}

no new diagnostics

+func (m *Model) openSettingsModal() {

+func (m *Model) handleSettingsModalKey(msg tea.KeyMsg) tea.Cmd {
+	switch msg.String() {
+	case "esc":
+		applyTheme(m.settingsOrigTheme)
+		m.settings.Theme = m.settingsOrigTheme
+		m.modal = modalNone
+		return nil
+	case "up", "k":
+		m.settingsCursor = (m.settingsCursor - 1 + 2) % 2
+		return nil
+	case "down", "j":
+		m.settingsCursor = (m.settingsCursor + 1) % 2
+		return nil
+	case "left", "h":
+		if m.settingsCursor == 0 {
+			n := len(themeOrder)
+			m.settingsThemeCursor = (m.settingsThemeCursor - 1 + n) % n
+			m.settings.Theme = themeOrder[m.settingsThemeCursor]
+			applyTheme(m.settings.Theme)
+		}
+		return nil
+	case "right", "l":
+		if m.settingsCursor == 0 {
+			n := len(themeOrder)
+			m.settingsThemeCursor = (m.settingsThemeCursor + 1) % n
+			m.settings.Theme = themeOrder[m.settingsThemeCursor]
+			applyTheme(m.settings.Theme)
+		}
+		return nil
+	case " ", "space":
+		if m.settingsCursor == 1 {
+			m.settings.MouseEnabled = !m.settings.MouseEnabled
+			if m.settings.MouseEnabled {
+				return tea.EnableMouseCellMotion
+			}
+			return tea.DisableMouseCellMotion
+		}
+		return nil
+	case "enter", "s":
+		_ = settings.Save(m.settings)
+		m.modal = modalNone
+		return nil
+	}
+	return nil
 }

package tea // import "github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea"

func EnableMouseCellMotion() Msg
    EnableMouseCellMotion is a special command that enables mouse click,
    release, and wheel events. Mouse movement events are also captured if a
    mouse button is pressed (i.e., drag events).

    Because commands run asynchronously, this command should not be used in your
    model's Init function. Use the WithMouseCellMotion ProgramOption instead.

    DisableMouse is a special command that stops listening for mouse events.

+func (m Model) viewSettingsModal() string {
+	rowStyle := func(active bool) lipgloss.Style {
+		if active {
+			return lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(focusC).Bold(true)
+		}
+		return dimStyle()
+	}
+
+	themeName := currentThemeName
+	if m.settingsThemeCursor >= 0 && m.settingsThemeCursor < len(themeOrder) {
+		themeName = themeOrder[m.settingsThemeCursor]
+	}
+	themeRow := rowStyle(m.settingsCursor == 0).Render(fmt.Sprintf("Theme:  < %s >", themeName))
+
+	check := "[ ]"
+	if m.settings.MouseEnabled {
+		check = "[x]"
+	}
+	mouseRow := rowStyle(m.settingsCursor == 1).Render(fmt.Sprintf("Mouse support:  %s", check))
+
+	hint := dimStyle().Render("↑/↓ select  ←/→ change  space toggle  enter save  esc cancel")
+	body := titleStyle().Render("Settings") + "\n\n" + themeRow + "\n" + mouseRow + "\n\n" + hint
